# ST Finding Solve Guide

Generated by `python -m tools.st2py.fix_guide` from the corrections of the transpiler - do not edit;
the hand written part comes from `tools/st2py/fix_guide_manual.md`.
Source: `third_party/robotlibrary/RobotLibrary.xml` (sha256 `6d064f48fb9cb9c5`).
Findings: [ST_FINDINGS.md](ST_FINDINGS.md).

## Instructions for the agent that fixes the PLC library

You fix the findings of the SRCI PLC library (Structured Text, Codesys/TwinCAT) that are already
fixed in the Python port. Every ST change below is **exactly** the change the Python port applies
to the ST text before it transpiles it, so the result is proven by the Python test suite
(unit tests, bilateral tests and the Siemens methodology against the SRCI SDK).

1. **Scope.** Change only what this guide shows. Do not reformat, rename or "improve" other code.
   Keep the comments `// ST-FIX Fnn` of the new code - they mark the fix in the library.
2. **Order.** Work finding by finding in the order of this guide and, inside a finding, step by
   step. The steps are cumulative: the context lines of a step already contain the earlier
   steps (also of earlier findings in the same method).
3. **Diffs.** Each step is a unified diff of one part of a POU (`declaration`, `body` or a
   method). The ST text comes from the XML export; indentation and blank lines in the `.st`
   files may differ - match the lines by content. `-` lines are removed, `+` lines added.
   Where a finding changes the same lines in many POUs, the diff is shown once and the other
   POUs are listed; apply the same change there (the context lines can differ slightly).
4. **Declarations.** "variables" steps add the shown `VAR…END_VAR` / `VAR_INPUT…END_VAR` block to
   the declaration of the POU (merge into an existing section of the same kind if you prefer).
5. **Clones.** "clone" steps replace a POU by a copy of another one (e.g. `MC_OpenBrakeFB` becomes
   an Enable block like `MC_FreeDriveFB`); the diffs show every part against the old POU. New
   methods appear as a diff against an empty text - create them.
6. **Types.** The section *Data types* lists changes of DUTs, enums and constants.
7. **Hand written parts.** The section *Fixes without generated diff* describes fixes that the
   Python port implements in hand written code; implement them as described.
8. **If a diff does not match** the current ST (the library changed since the export): do not
   guess. Stop that finding and report the POU, method and the diff.
9. **Verification** (in the SRCI_PY repository after the fixes):
   1. export the library as PLCopen XML to `third_party/robotlibrary/RobotLibrary.xml`;
   2. `python -m tools.plcopen_gen` - every type override that is now in the XML fails as
      "obsolete": remove it from `tools/plcopen_gen/overrides.py` and run again;
   3. `python -m tools.st2py` - every source patch whose fix is now in the ST fails as
      "obsolete (text not found / no match)": remove it from `tools/st2py/config.py`
      (also `VarAppend`/`BodyAppend`/`PouClone` that now fail) and run again until no error;
   4. `git diff src/` must show no functional change (only comments/whitespace);
   5. `pytest` (with the SDK: `SRCI_SDK_SIM_LIB=…`) - everything green;
   6. `python -m tools.st2py.fix_guide` - the guide then only lists what is still open.

## Generated ST changes

### F12

_`MC_MeasuringInputFB.CheckParameterValid` - `SetError( ErrorID := ErrorID := ...)` – duplicated assignment in the argument list_

- F12: duplicated 'ErrorID :=' in the argument list (assignment expression)

**MC_MeasuringInputFB** · `CheckParameterValid` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_MeasuringInputFB.CheckParameterValid
+++ b/MC_MeasuringInputFB.CheckParameterValid
@@ -10,7 +10,7 @@
   // Parameter not valid
   CheckParameterValid := FALSE;
   // Set error
-  SetError( ErrorID := ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_INVALID_PAR_CMD, Overwrite := TRUE );
+  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_INVALID_PAR_CMD, Overwrite := TRUE );
   // Create log entry
   CreateLogMessagePara1 ( Timestamp   := AxesGroup.State.SystemTime,
                           MessageType := MessageType.CMD,
```

### F13

_`MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleSync` - `HandleSyncToolData` is called twice per cycle (the tool step chain runs at double speed, e.g. `Execute` set and evaluated in the same cycle)_

- F13: HandleSyncToolData was called twice per cycle

**MC_RobotTaskFB** · `HandleSync` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleSync
+++ b/MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleSync
@@ -4,7 +4,7 @@
 HandleSyncRobotDefaultDynamics  ( AxesGroup := AxesGroup, DefaultDynamics   := DefaultDynamics);
 HandleSyncRobotReferenceDynamics( AxesGroup := AxesGroup, ReferenceDynamics := ReferenceDynamics);
 HandleSyncRobotSWLimits         ( AxesGroup := AxesGroup, SwLimits          := SwLimits);
-HandleSyncToolData              ( AxesGroup := AxesGroup, ToolData          := ToolData);
+// ST-FIX F13: HandleSyncToolData was called a second time here
 HandleSyncWorkArea              ( AxesGroup := AxesGroup, WorkAreas         := WorkAreas);
 
 
```

### F14

_`MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleAxesGroupSystemData` - `FrameDataPtr`/`WorkAreasPtr` (and Min/Max) are set to **LoadData** (copy & paste) – frame and work area data are written into the load array_

- F14: FrameData of the system data pointed to LoadData (copy & paste)
- F14: WorkAreas of the system data pointed to LoadData (copy & paste)

**MC_RobotTaskFB** · `HandleAxesGroupSystemData` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleAxesGroupSystemData
+++ b/MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleAxesGroupSystemData
@@ -11,7 +11,7 @@
 AxesGroup.SystemData.LoadDataCount     := DINT_TO_UDINT(1 + AxesGroup.SystemData.LoadDataMax - AxesGroup.SystemData.LoadDataMin);
                                        
 // Update FrameData       
-AxesGroup.SystemData.FrameDataPtr      := LoadData;                                                                       
+AxesGroup.SystemData.FrameDataPtr      := FrameData;
```

### F15

_`MC_RobotTaskFB.OnExecRun` step 1 - timeout check `(State >= ERROR_161) OR (State <= ERROR_173)` is always TRUE → `ErrorID := TelegramState` also for non-error states_

- F15: the range check with OR was always TRUE -> ErrorID := TelegramState also for states without error

**MC_RobotTaskFB** · `OnExecRun` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_RobotTaskFB.OnExecRun
+++ b/MC_RobotTaskFB.OnExecRun
@@ -95,7 +95,7 @@
        IF (CheckTimeout(_timerCmd) = RobotLibraryConstants.OK)
        THEN
          // Check Telegram State error ? 
-         IF (( AxesGroup.Cyclic.RobToPlc.TelegramState >= TelegramState.ERROR_161_TELEGRAM_CONTROL_MISMATCH_TELEGRAM_STATE ) OR
+         IF (( AxesGroup.Cyclic.RobToPlc.TelegramState >= TelegramState.ERROR_161_TELEGRAM_CONTROL_MISMATCH_TELEGRAM_STATE ) AND // ST-FIX F15
              ( AxesGroup.Cyclic.RobToPlc.TelegramState <= TelegramState.ERROR_173_SERVER_CONNECTION_LOST                   ))
          THEN
            ErrorID     := AxesGroup.Cyclic.RobToPlc.TelegramState;
```

### F16

_`MC_Read*FB` (tool/frame/load/SW limits/dynamics) during the synchronisation - the start-up read calls `AxesGroup.SystemData.Update*` and so **overwrites the user data with the RC data** before the comparison – with `CLIENT_TO_SERVER` the PLC data is lost and the comparison always finds equal data_

- F16: the internal read commands of the synchronisation overwrote the user data with the RC data before the comparison (and on error with the undefined OutCmd)

**MC_ReadToolDataFB** · `declaration` · variables

```diff
+VAR_INPUT
+  UpdateSystemData : BOOL := TRUE; // ST-FIX F16: FALSE for the internal instances of MC_RobotTaskFB (synchronisation)
+END_VAR
```

**MC_ReadFrameDataFB** · `declaration` · variables

```diff
+VAR_INPUT
+  UpdateSystemData : BOOL := TRUE; // ST-FIX F16: FALSE for the internal instances of MC_RobotTaskFB (synchronisation)
+END_VAR
```

**MC_ReadLoadDataFB** · `declaration` · variables

```diff
+VAR_INPUT
+  UpdateSystemData : BOOL := TRUE; // ST-FIX F16: FALSE for the internal instances of MC_RobotTaskFB (synchronisation)
+END_VAR
```

Same change (same `-`/`+` lines) in: `MC_ReadWorkAreaFB`, `MC_ReadRobotSWLimitsFB`, `MC_ReadRobotDefaultDynamicsFB`, `MC_ReadRobotReferenceDynamicsFB`

**MC_ReadToolDataFB** · `OnExecRun` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_ReadToolDataFB.OnExecRun
+++ b/MC_ReadToolDataFB.OnExecRun
@@ -43,10 +43,13 @@
          IF (_response.State >= CmdMessageState.DONE )
          THEN           
            // Update the ToolData in user defined system datas 
-           AxesGroup.SystemData.UpdateToolData( Caller     := THIS^,
+           IF UpdateSystemData AND ( _response.State = CmdMessageState.DONE ) // ST-FIX F16
+THEN
+AxesGroup.SystemData.UpdateToolData( Caller     := THIS^,
                                                 SystemTime := AxesGroup.State.SystemTime,
                                                 ToolNo     := OutCmd.ToolNoReturn, 
-                                                ToolData   := OutCmd.ToolData);                     
+                                                ToolData   := OutCmd.ToolData);
+END_IF                     
            // set timeout
            SetTimeout(PT := _timeoutCmd, rTimer := _timerCmd);
            // inc step counter
```

### F17

_`MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleAxesGroupState` - `Unified*Index := MIN(..., X_MAX, ...)` but the internal arrays are `[0..X_MAX-1]` → write/read behind `_toolData` etc._

- F17: internal arrays are [0..FRAME_MAX-1], the unified index must not exceed FRAME_MAX-1
- F17: internal arrays are [0..TOOL_MAX-1], the unified index must not exceed TOOL_MAX-1
- F17: internal arrays are [0..LOAD_MAX-1], the unified index must not exceed LOAD_MAX-1
- F17: internal arrays are [0..WORK_AREAS_MAX-1], the unified index must not exceed WORK_AREAS_MAX-1

### F18

_`MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleSync*Data` - copy loops run over the user array bounds (`ToolDataMin..ToolDataMax`) on the internal arrays `[0..TOOL_MAX-1]` → out of bounds if the user array is longer than `TOOL_MAX` (Python raises `IndexError`)_

- F18: the copy loop ran over the bounds of the user array on the internal array [0..FRAME_MAX-1]
- F18: the copy loop ran over the bounds of the user array on the internal array [0..LOAD_MAX-1]
- F18: the copy loop ran over the bounds of the user array on the internal array [0..TOOL_MAX-1]
- F18: the copy loop ran over the bounds of the user array on the internal array [0..WORK_AREAS_MAX-1]

**MC_RobotTaskFB** · `HandleSyncFrameData` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleSyncFrameData
+++ b/MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleSyncFrameData
@@ -189,7 +189,7 @@
 
            SyncMode.SERVER_TO_CLIENT : 
 
-             FOR _idx := AxesGroup.SystemData.FrameDataMin TO AxesGroup.SystemData.FrameDataMax
+             FOR _idx := AxesGroup.SystemData.FrameDataMin TO MIN(AxesGroup.SystemData.FrameDataMax, RobotLibraryParameter.FRAME_MAX - 1) // ST-FIX F18
              DO
                // Overwrite PLC data with RC data
                FrameData[_idx] := _frameData[_idx];            
```

### F19

_`ActiveCommandRegisterFB.AddRsp` - `FOR _idx := PayloadPointer TO PayloadLength - 1` – for fragments after the first one (`PayloadPointer > 0`) nothing is copied, long responses (e.g. `ReadRobotData`, 134 bytes) are truncated_

- F19: fragments after the first one (PayloadPointer > 0) were not copied into the response buffer

**ActiveCommandRegisterFB** · `AddRsp` · patch

```diff
--- a/ActiveCommandRegisterFB.AddRsp
+++ b/ActiveCommandRegisterFB.AddRsp
@@ -14,7 +14,7 @@
     END_IF
                        
     // add payload to response
-    FOR _idx := Rsp.Header.PayloadPointer TO  Rsp.Header.PayloadLength -1
+    FOR _idx := Rsp.Header.PayloadPointer TO  Rsp.Header.PayloadPointer + Rsp.Header.PayloadLength -1
     DO
       Register[_regIdx].Response[ACTIVE_CMD].Timestamp     := SystemTime;
       Register[_regIdx].Response[ACTIVE_CMD].State         := BufferStateRsp.RECEIVING;
```

### F20

_synchronisation `SERVER_TO_CLIENT` - after reading the RC data the client never writes it back, so `DataChanged` on the RC is not reset and the RC never reports `DataInSync` → RI state never "Synchronized"_

- F20: SERVER_TO_CLIENT never reset DataChanged on the RC -> RC never in sync

**MC_RobotTaskFB** · `HandleSyncToolData` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleSyncToolData
+++ b/MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleSyncToolData
@@ -641,7 +641,8 @@
          // set timeout
          SetTimeout(PT := _rTimeout, rTimer := _rTimer);
          // inc step counter
-        _rStep := 10; // -> jump to after startup           
+        // ST-FIX F20: write the RC data back to reset DataChanged on the RC (spec 5.6.7.4.2)
+IF AxesGroup.State.RobotData.RCSupportedFunctions.WriteToolData THEN _rStep := 30; ELSE _rStep := 10; END_IF // -> jump to after startup           
        ELSE
          // check error ? 
          IF (_readToolData.Error)
```

```diff
          // check error ?
```

### F21

_synchronisation `CLIENT_TO_SERVER` - writes tool/frame/load **0** (flange, world, …); the RC rejects it (SDK `0x8D35` invalid tool number) → warning, sync fails_

- F21: the synchronisation wrote tool 0 (fixed on the RC, spec 5.5.4) - as LoadData
- F21: the synchronisation wrote frame 0 (fixed on the RC, spec 5.5.4) - as LoadData

```diff
        // set timeout
```

### F23

_`RobotLibraryBaseEnableFB` / `MC_RobotTaskFB.Reset` - disabling the RobotTask starts the cancel of the enable FBs (`_cancel`, 5 s timeout); a new enable within this time lets the pending cancel reset `MC_ReadMessagesFB` after its first response → start-up hangs in step 3_

- F23: a restart while the RC was still initialized kept the old ACR and SEQ/ACK on the RC
- F23: a pending cancel (5 s) of the previous enable reset the block after the new enable

**MC_RobotTaskFB** · `declaration` · variables

```diff
+VAR
+  _restartReset : BOOL; // ST-FIX F23: interface reset on the RC requested by a restart
+END_VAR
```

**MC_RobotTaskFB** · `OnExecRun` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_RobotTaskFB.OnExecRun
+++ b/MC_RobotTaskFB.OnExecRun
@@ -31,6 +31,10 @@
          AxesGroup.Cyclic.PlcToRob.FastStop := 0;
          // Reset Active command register
          AxesGroup.Acyclic.ActiveCommandRegister.Reset();
+         // ST-FIX F23: the RC is still initialized from the previous enable (ACR, SEQ/ACK)
+         // -> reset the interface on the RC as well
+         _restartReset := AxesGroup.Cyclic.RobToPlc.TelegramState = TelegramState.INITIALIZED;
+         IF _restartReset THEN AxesGroup.Cyclic.PlcToRob.Control := ControlHalfByte.RESET; END_IF
        
          // check parameter valid
          IF ( CheckParameterValid(AxesGroup := AxesGroup) )
```

**MC_RobotTaskFB** · `OnExecRun` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_RobotTaskFB.OnExecRun
+++ b/MC_RobotTaskFB.OnExecRun
@@ -46,7 +46,11 @@
          END_IF
        END_IF
      
-  01:  CASE AxesGroup.Cyclic.RobToPlc.TelegramState  OF
+  01:  // ST-FIX F23: wait until the RC left the state INITIALIZED of the previous enable
+IF _restartReset THEN
+  IF AxesGroup.Cyclic.RobToPlc.TelegramState <> TelegramState.INITIALIZED THEN _restartReset := FALSE; END_IF
+ELSE
+CASE AxesGroup.Cyclic.RobToPlc.TelegramState  OF
 
          TelegramState.UNDEFINED : ;
          
@@ -94,6 +98,7 @@
          SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_INIT_LOST_UNKNOWN_0x80A2, Overwrite := TRUE ); // ST-FIX F65: unknown state (16#A2 is the RI error of the RC)
          ErrorAddTxt := CONCAT('_stepCmd = ' , DINT_TO_STRING(_stepCmd));
        END_CASE
+END_IF
 
        // timeout exceeded ? 
        IF (CheckTimeout(_timerCmd) = RobotLibraryConstants.OK)
```

**RobotLibraryBaseEnableFB** · `OnCall` · patch

```diff
--- a/RobotLibraryBaseEnableFB.OnCall
+++ b/RobotLibraryBaseEnableFB.OnCall
@@ -22,6 +22,11 @@
 // On execution started
 IF ( _enable_R.Q )
 THEN
+  // ST-FIX F23: a new enable ends a pending cancel / error clear of the previous enable
+  _cancel := FALSE;
+  _stepCancel := 0;
+  _clearError := FALSE;
+  _stepClearError := 0;
   OnExecStart(AxesGroup := AxesGroup);
 END_IF
 
```

### F28

_`CheckAddParameter` of 32 FBs (list `F28_POUS` in `tools/st2py/config.py`) - a parameter is omitted when the bytes of `_command` **behind the payload position** are zero; this assumes payload order = structure layout. Where it differs non-zero parameters are dropped, e.g. `WriteToolData`: `ToolNo` (payload end, structure start) not sent → SDK 0x8D35; `WriteRobotSWLimits`: upper limits not sent (structure J1Lower, J1Upper, …; payload all lower, then all upper); `MC_EnableRobotFB.HoldToRun`; `E2..E6` of positions_

- F28: CheckAddParameter omits non-zero parameters when the payload order differs from _command

**MC_CalculateInverseKinematicFB** · `CheckAddParameter` · append

```diff
--- a/MC_CalculateInverseKinematicFB.CheckAddParameter
+++ b/MC_CalculateInverseKinematicFB.CheckAddParameter
@@ -6,4 +6,6 @@
 DataLen := LIMIT(1, SIZEOF(Payload) - PayloadPtr, SIZEOF(Payload)); 
 // Compare Payload-Array with Null-Byte-Array
 CheckAddParameter := SysDepMemCmp( pData1 := ADR(Payload) + PayloadPtr , pData2 := ADR(Null), DataLen := DataLen) <>  RobotLibraryConstants.OK ;
-                
+
+// ST-FIX F28: payload order differs from the structure layout -> always add the parameter
+CheckAddParameter := TRUE;
```

### F33

_payload of 24 extended/optional FBs - layout differs from the spec tables (the bilateral test also found `MC_LoadMeasurementAutomaticFB`: Position_1 complete before Position_2) (shifted by missing/additional bytes, wrong data types, off-by-one loops). List with details: `KNOWN` in `tests/unit/fb/test_payload_spec.py`_

- F33/F45: DynamicSpline is an Enable block in the spec that transmits one spline point per parameter update (ParSeq); the ST block sent all points in one payload (> 255 bytes) with Execute; state machine of MC_FreeDriveFB, payload of MC_DynamicSplineFB
- F33: one spline point per parameter update
- F33: one command per spline point
- F33: bits of PositiveJointDirection
- F33: AddArmConfig writes 2 bytes (config + reserved), the additional Reserve byte shifted E1..E6
- F33: 4 bytes after Time are not in the spec
- F33: Reserved byte before Time (spec table 6-310) was missing
- F33: Reserved byte after ListenerID (spec table 6-607) was missing
- F33: Reserved byte after ListenerID (spec table 6-696) was missing
- F33: ConfigMode, TurnMode, Reserved and Time (spec table 6-348) were missing
- F33: Time (spec table 6-563) was missing
- F33: Manipulation was sent before BlendingParameter (spec table 6-545: byte 70)
- F33: TriggerDelay .. TurnMode (spec table 6-545 bytes 58..71) were not sent
- F33: ReferenceType was sent as UINT
- F33: Config and TurnNumber of Position (spec table 6-545 bytes 48..53) were not sent
- F33: TargetWindow (spec table 6-741 byte 82) was not sent
- F33: Reserved byte 15 after Index (spec table 6-512) was missing
- F33: Reserved byte 21 after OutputBitmask (spec table 6-512) was missing
- F33: Values were sent as REAL
- F33: Values/Index are [0..6] (spec table 6-530), the loops ran from 1
- F33: DefinitionMode was removed from the work area data in the spec (change log, table 6-229)
- F33: DefinitionMode was removed from the work area data in the spec (change log)
- F33: ToolData.Date follows TCPMaxError/TCPMeanError (spec table 6-673)
- F33: ForceStatus was read before InvocationCounter as well
- F33: PositiveJointDirection was read as 7 bytes, the spec has 7 bits of one byte
- F33: ActivationState/MonitoringState are bit 0/1 of one WORD (spec table 6-242)
- F33: the Reserved byte before ReturnData was not skipped -> ReturnData shifted by one
- F33: Position_1.E2..E6 follow Position_2.E1 (spec: J1..E1 of both positions, then E2..E6)
- F33: RCParameter (last byte, spec table 6-648) was never sent
- F33: the payload of CreateSpline has one SplineData (spec table 6-776); all 64 points were written into one payload (> 255 bytes, behind the buffer)
- F33: the payload of DynamicSpline has one SplineData (spec); all 64 points were written
- F33: number of spline points (one command per point)
- F33: number of spline points (one parameter update per point)

**MC_DynamicSplineFB** · `OnExecCancel` · clone

```diff
--- a/MC_DynamicSplineFB.OnExecCancel
+++ b/MC_DynamicSplineFB.OnExecCancel
@@ -0,0 +1,90 @@
+                METHOD Protected OnExecCancel : DINT
+VAR_IN_OUT
+  /// Robot assignment of function
+  AxesGroup : AxesGroup; 
+END_VAR
+VAR
+  /// internal return value 
+ _retVal    : DINT;
+END_VAR
+
+              
+
+                  OnExecCancel := RobotLibraryConstants.RUNNING;
+
+CASE _stepCancel OF
+  
+  0: // set busy flag
+     Busy := TRUE;
+    
+     // Create log entry
+     CreateLogMessagePara1 ( Timestamp   := AxesGroup.State.SystemTime,
+                             MessageType := MessageType.CMD,
+                             Severity    := Severity.DEBUG,
+                             MessageCode := 0,
+                             MessageText := 'Execution of {1} cancelled',
+                             Para1       := MyType);                 
+    
+     // try to remove cmd
+    _retVal := AxesGroup.Acyclic.ActiveCommandRegister.RemoveCmd(_uniqueID);
+  
+     // check result of removement       
+     IF ( _retVal = RobotLibraryConstants.OK )
+     THEN
+       // Reset step counter
+      _stepCancel := 0;
+       // finished okay
+       OnExecCancel := RobotLibraryConstants.OK;
+     
+       // Create log entry
+       CreateLogMessagePara1 ( Timestamp   := AxesGroup.State.SystemTime,
+                               MessageType := MessageType.CMD,
+                               Severity    := Severity.DEBUG,
+                               MessageCode := 0,
+                               MessageText := '{1} successfully removed from ACR',
+                               Para1       := MyType);                 
+     ELSE
+       // set timeout
+       SetTimeout(PT := _timeoutCancel, rTimer := _timerCancel);
+       // inc step counter
+      _stepCancel := _stepCancel + 1; 
+     
+       // Create log entry
+       CreateLogMessagePara1 ( Timestamp   := AxesGroup.State.SystemTime,
+                               MessageType := MessageType.CMD,
+                               Severity    := Severity.DEBUG,
+                               MessageCode := 0,
+                               MessageText := '{1} was not removed from ACR because execution was already in progress',
+                               Para1       := MyType);                 
+     END_IF       
+  
+     
+ 1:  // call clear error 
+     OnExecCancel := OnExecErrorClear(AxesGroup := AxesGroup);  
+ 
+     IF ( OnExecCancel = RobotLibraryConstants.OK)
+     THEN
+       // Reset busy flag
+       Busy := FALSE;
+       // Reset step counter
+      _stepCancel := 0;
+       // finished okay
+       OnExecCancel := RobotLibraryConstants.OK;
+     END_IF  
+  
+ELSE
+  // invalid step
+  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_INVALID_STEP, Overwrite := TRUE );
+END_CASE  
+
+
+                        
+// reset step counter
+IF (OnExecCancel <> RobotLibraryConstants.RUNNING)
+THEN
+  // Reset FB variables
+  Reset();
+  // Reset step counter
+ _stepCancel := 0;
+END_IF                        
+                
```

**MC_DynamicSplineFB** · `OnExecErrorClear` · clone

```diff
--- a/MC_DynamicSplineFB.OnExecErrorClear
+++ b/MC_DynamicSplineFB.OnExecErrorClear
@@ -0,0 +1,54 @@
+                METHOD PROTECTED OnExecErrorClear : DINT
+VAR_IN_OUT
+  /// Robot assignment of function
+  AxesGroup : AxesGroup; 
+END_VAR
+
+
+              
+
+                  OnExecErrorClear := RobotLibraryConstants.RUNNING;
+
+CASE _stepClearError OF
+  
+  0:  // set busy flag
+      Busy := TRUE;
+      // trigger parameter update to disable FB
+     _parameterUpdateInternal := TRUE;
+      // call Check Parameter changed method to trigger the parameter update to disable the function
+      CheckParameterChanged(AxesGroup := AxesGroup);
+      // set timeout
+      SetTimeout(PT := _timeoutClearError, rTimer := _timerClearError);
+      // inc step counter
+     _stepClearError := _stepClearError + 1; 
+      
+  1: IF ( _responseReceived )
+     THEN
+       // reset response received flag
+      _responseReceived := FALSE;
+       // reset step counter
+      _stepClearError := 0;
+       // finished
+       OnExecErrorClear := RobotLibraryConstants.OK;
+     ELSE
+       // timeout exceeded ? 
+       IF (CheckTimeout(_timerClearError) = RobotLibraryConstants.OK)
+       THEN
+         OnExecErrorClear := RobotLibraryConstants.HAS_ERROR;
+       END_IF
+	   END_IF
+ELSE
+  // invalid step
+  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_INVALID_STEP, Overwrite := TRUE );
+END_CASE
+
+
+// reset step counter
+IF (OnExecErrorClear <> RobotLibraryConstants.RUNNING)
+THEN
+  // Reset 
+  Reset();     
+  // reset step counter
+ _stepClearError := 0;
+END_IF
+                
```

**MC_DynamicSplineFB** · `OnUpdateStateFlags` · clone

```diff
--- a/MC_DynamicSplineFB.OnUpdateStateFlags

@@ -6,9 +6,20 @@
               
 
                   // Reset State flags
-Active             := FALSE;
-CommandAborted     := FALSE;
-CommandInterrupted := FALSE;
+;
+
+// Update Enable flag
+// ST-FIX F33/F45: enable block, spline points are sent one by one
+Enabled := ( State = CmdMessageState.ACTIVE ) OR ( State = CmdMessageState.BUFFERED );
+Active  := ( State = CmdMessageState.ACTIVE );
+// next spline point (the point after the last one is empty: end of the trajectory)
+IF ( _response.ParSeq = _command.ParSeq ) AND ( _pointIndex <= _pointCount ) AND
+   ( _pointIndex < RobotLibraryParameter.SPLINE_DATA_MAX ) AND
+   (( State = CmdMessageState.ACTIVE ) OR ( State = CmdMessageState.BUFFERED ))
+THEN
+  _pointIndex := _pointIndex + 1;
+  _parameterUpdateInternal := TRUE;
+END_IF
 
 CASE State OF
 
@@ -23,19 +34,17 @@

+  CmdMessageState.ACTIVE              :
```

**MC_ReadDHParameterFB** · `ParseResponsePayload` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_ReadDHParameterFB.ParseResponsePayload
+++ b/MC_ReadDHParameterFB.ParseResponsePayload
@@ -68,17 +68,16 @@
   END_IF
 END_FOR
 
-FOR _idx := 0 TO 6
-DO
-  // Check payload remaining ? 
-  IF ( ResponseData.IsPayloadRemaining)
-  THEN  
-    // Get Response.DHParameter.PositiveJointDirection[_idx]
-   _response.DHParameter.PositiveJointDirection[_idx] := ResponseData.GetBool();
-    // inc parameter counter
-   _parameterCnt := _parameterCnt + 1;
-  END_IF
-END_FOR
+// ST-FIX F33: PositiveJointDirection[0..6] are the bits 0..6 of one byte (spec table)
+IF ( ResponseData.IsPayloadRemaining )
+THEN
+  _directionBits := ResponseData.GetByte();
+  FOR _idx := 0 TO 6
+  DO
+    _response.DHParameter.PositiveJointDirection[_idx] := ( ( SHR(_directionBits, _idx) AND 1 ) = 1 );
+  END_FOR
+  _parameterCnt := _parameterCnt + 1;
+END_IF
```

### F34

_`MC_UserLoginFB`, `MC_SwitchLanguageFB` - `AddString` writes only the actual length; the spec has fixed fields (`Password`/`Username` 50, `LanguageCode` 2) → `Username` at the wrong offset_

- F34: Password was sent with its actual length instead of the field length 50
- F34: Username was sent with its actual length instead of the field length 50
- F34: LanguageCode was sent with its actual length instead of the field length 2

**MC_UserLoginFB** · `CreateCommandPayload` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_UserLoginFB.CreateCommandPayload
+++ b/MC_UserLoginFB.CreateCommandPayload
@@ -40,7 +40,8 @@
 IF ( CheckAddParameter(CreateCommandPayload.PayloadPtr))
 THEN
   // add command.Password
-  CreateCommandPayload.AddString(_command.Password);
+  // ST-FIX F34: Password is a field of 50 characters (spec table 6-69), padded with 0
+CreateCommandPayload.AddDataBlock(pValue := ADR(_command.Password), Size := 50);
   // inc parameter counter
  _parameterCnt := _parameterCnt + 1;  
 END_IF
```

**MC_UserLoginFB** · `CreateCommandPayload` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_UserLoginFB.CreateCommandPayload
+++ b/MC_UserLoginFB.CreateCommandPayload
@@ -51,7 +51,8 @@
 IF ( CheckAddParameter(CreateCommandPayload.PayloadPtr))
 THEN
   // add command.Username
-  CreateCommandPayload.AddString(_command.Username);
+  // ST-FIX F34: Username is a field of 50 characters (spec table 6-69), padded with 0
+CreateCommandPayload.AddDataBlock(pValue := ADR(_command.Username), Size := 50);
   // inc parameter counter
  _parameterCnt := _parameterCnt + 1;  
 END_IF
```

**MC_SwitchLanguageFB** · `CreateCommandPayload` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_SwitchLanguageFB.CreateCommandPayload
+++ b/MC_SwitchLanguageFB.CreateCommandPayload
@@ -17,7 +17,8 @@
 IF ( CheckAddParameter(CreateCommandPayload.PayloadPtr))
 THEN
   // add command.LanguageCode
-  CreateCommandPayload.AddString(_command.LanguageCode);
+  // ST-FIX F34: LanguageCode is a field of 2 characters (spec table 6-76), padded with 0
```

### F37

_`MC_EnableRobotFB`, `MC_MoveCircularRelativeFB` `.CreateCommandPayload` - two BOOLs that are bit 0/1 of **one** byte are sent as two bytes (`HoldToRun`/`ManualStep`, `PathChoice`/`Manipulation`) → `ManualStep`/`Manipulation` land in a reserved byte (not visible in the layout check, sizes happen to fit)_

- F37: HoldToRun/ManualStep are bit 0/1 of one byte (spec table 6-25), ST sent 2 bytes
- F37: PathChoice/Manipulation are bit 0/1 of one byte (spec table 6-357), ST sent 2 bytes
- F37: PathChoice/Manipulation are bit 0/1 of one byte (spec table 6-348), ST sent 2 bytes

```diff
@@ -39,7 +39,7 @@

 THEN
   // add command.HoldToRun
-  CreateCommandPayload.AddBool(_command.HoldToRun);
+  CreateCommandPayload.AddByte(BOOL_TO_BYTE(_command.HoldToRun) OR SHL(BOOL_TO_BYTE(_command.ManualStep), 1)); // ST-FIX F37
   // inc parameter counter
  _parameterCnt := _parameterCnt + 1;  
 END_IF
```

**MC_EnableRobotFB** · `CreateCommandPayload` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_EnableRobotFB.CreateCommandPayload

 THEN
   // add command.ManualStep
-  CreateCommandPayload.AddBool(_command.ManualStep);                      
+  CreateCommandPayload.AddByte(0); // ST-FIX F37: reserved byte, the value is bit 1 of the byte before                      
   // inc parameter counter
  _parameterCnt := _parameterCnt + 1;  
 END_IF
```

### F42

_`MC_OpenBrakeFB` - Execute block; the spec defines OpenBrake with **Enable** (brakes stay open while Enable)_

- F42: OpenBrake is an Enable block in the spec (brakes open while Enable), the ST block was an Execute block; state machine of MC_FreeDriveFB, payload of MC_OpenBrakeFB
- F42: Enable byte of the OpenBrake command
- F42: Enabled byte of the OpenBrake response
- F42: the results of an enable block are updated while it is active
- F42: Enabled output of the enable block

**MC_OpenBrakeFB** · `declaration` · clone

```diff
--- a/MC_OpenBrakeFB.declaration
+++ b/MC_OpenBrakeFB.declaration

+              /// Release robot arm's brakes (Enable block, ST-FIX F42)
+FUNCTION_BLOCK MC_OpenBrakeFB EXTENDS RobotLibraryBaseEnableFB
 // ------------------------------------------------------------------------- 
 //  SRCI Robot Library                                                
 // ------------------------------------------------------------------------- 
 //                                                                           
-//  Object:      OpenBrakeFB                                       
+//  Object:      FreeDriveFB                                       
 //  Author:      Thorsten Brach                                                
 //  Date:        2024-06-01
 //
@@ -24,7 +24,7 @@
 // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 VAR_INPUT
   /// Command parameter 
-  ParCmd             : OpenBrakeParCmd;  
+  ParCmd            : OpenBrakeParCmd;
 END_VAR
 
 VAR_OUTPUT
@@ -32,17 +32,18 @@
   CommandBuffered   : BOOL;
   /// Receiving of input parameter values has been acknowledged by RC
   ParameterAccepted : BOOL;
-  /// Command output 
-  OutCmd            : OpenBrakeOutCmd;  
+  /// command results
+  OutCmd            : OpenBrakeOutCmd;
 END_VAR
 
 VAR
   /// internal copy of command parameter 
- _parCmd             : OpenBrakeParCmd;
+ _parCmd            : OpenBrakeParCmd;
   /// command data to send
- _command            : OpenBrakeSendData;
+ _command           : OpenBrakeSendData;
   /// response data received
- _response           : OpenBrakeRecvData;      
+ _response          : OpenBrakeRecvData;
 END_VAR
+
             
           
```

**MC_OpenBrakeFB** · `OnExecCancel` · clone

```diff
--- a/MC_OpenBrakeFB.OnExecCancel
+++ b/MC_OpenBrakeFB.OnExecCancel
@@ -0,0 +1,90 @@
+                METHOD Protected OnExecCancel : DINT
+VAR_IN_OUT
+  /// Robot assignment of function
+  AxesGroup : AxesGroup; 
+END_VAR
+VAR
+  /// internal return value 
+ _retVal    : DINT;
+END_VAR
+
+              
+
+                  OnExecCancel := RobotLibraryConstants.RUNNING;
+
+CASE _stepCancel OF
+  
+  0: // set busy flag
+     Busy := TRUE;
+    
+     // Create log entry
+     CreateLogMessagePara1 ( Timestamp   := AxesGroup.State.SystemTime,
+                             MessageType := MessageType.CMD,
+                             Severity    := Severity.DEBUG,
+                             MessageCode := 0,
+                             MessageText := 'Execution of {1} cancelled',
+                             Para1       := MyType);                 
+    
+     // try to remove cmd
+    _retVal := AxesGroup.Acyclic.ActiveCommandRegister.RemoveCmd(_uniqueID);
+  
+     // check result of removement       
+     IF ( _retVal = RobotLibraryConstants.OK )
+     THEN
+       // Reset step counter
+      _stepCancel := 0;
+       // finished okay
+       OnExecCancel := RobotLibraryConstants.OK;
+     
+       // Create log entry
+       CreateLogMessagePara1 ( Timestamp   := AxesGroup.State.SystemTime,
+                               MessageType := MessageType.CMD,
+                               Severity    := Severity.DEBUG,
+                               MessageCode := 0,
+                               MessageText := '{1} successfully removed from ACR',
+                               Para1       := MyType);                 
+     ELSE
+       // set timeout
+       SetTimeout(PT := _timeoutCancel, rTimer := _timerCancel);
+       // inc step counter
+      _stepCancel := _stepCancel + 1; 
+     
+       // Create log entry
+       CreateLogMessagePara1 ( Timestamp   := AxesGroup.State.SystemTime,
+                               MessageType := MessageType.CMD,
+                               Severity    := Severity.DEBUG,
+                               MessageCode := 0,
+                               MessageText := '{1} was not removed from ACR because execution was already in progress',
+                               Para1       := MyType);                 
+     END_IF       
+  
+     
+ 1:  // call clear error 
+     OnExecCancel := OnExecErrorClear(AxesGroup := AxesGroup);  
+ 
+     IF ( OnExecCancel = RobotLibraryConstants.OK)
+     THEN
+       // Reset busy flag
+       Busy := FALSE;
+       // Reset step counter
+      _stepCancel := 0;
+       // finished okay
+       OnExecCancel := RobotLibraryConstants.OK;
+     END_IF  
+  
+ELSE
+  // invalid step
+  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_INVALID_STEP, Overwrite := TRUE );
+END_CASE  
+
+
+                        
+// reset step counter
+IF (OnExecCancel <> RobotLibraryConstants.RUNNING)
+THEN
+  // Reset FB variables
+  Reset();
+  // Reset step counter
+ _stepCancel := 0;
+END_IF                        
+                
```

**MC_OpenBrakeFB** · `OnExecErrorClear` · clone

```diff
--- a/MC_OpenBrakeFB.OnExecErrorClear
+++ b/MC_OpenBrakeFB.OnExecErrorClear
@@ -0,0 +1,54 @@
+                METHOD PROTECTED OnExecErrorClear : DINT
+VAR_IN_OUT
+  /// Robot assignment of function
+  AxesGroup : AxesGroup; 
+END_VAR
+
+
+              
+
+                  OnExecErrorClear := RobotLibraryConstants.RUNNING;
+
+CASE _stepClearError OF
+  
+  0:  // set busy flag
+      Busy := TRUE;
+      // trigger parameter update to disable FB
+     _parameterUpdateInternal := TRUE;
+      // call Check Parameter changed method to trigger the parameter update to disable the function
+      CheckParameterChanged(AxesGroup := AxesGroup);
+      // set timeout
+      SetTimeout(PT := _timeoutClearError, rTimer := _timerClearError);
+      // inc step counter
+     _stepClearError := _stepClearError + 1; 
+      
+  1: IF ( _responseReceived )
+     THEN
+       // reset response received flag
+      _responseReceived := FALSE;
+       // reset step counter
+      _stepClearError := 0;
+       // finished
+       OnExecErrorClear := RobotLibraryConstants.OK;
+     ELSE
+       // timeout exceeded ? 
+       IF (CheckTimeout(_timerClearError) = RobotLibraryConstants.OK)
+       THEN
+         OnExecErrorClear := RobotLibraryConstants.HAS_ERROR;
+       END_IF
+	   END_IF
+ELSE
+  // invalid step
+  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_INVALID_STEP, Overwrite := TRUE );
+END_CASE
+
+
+// reset step counter
+IF (OnExecErrorClear <> RobotLibraryConstants.RUNNING)
+THEN
+  // Reset 
+  Reset();     
+  // reset step counter
+ _stepClearError := 0;
+END_IF
+                
```

### F50

_base of all Execute blocks - a falling edge of `Execute` before the end cancels the command: Execute for one cycle → nothing is sent; reset during the motion → motion runs, but `Done` is never shown_

- F50: Execute of the caller and internal hold of Execute while Busy
- F50: a falling edge of Execute before the end cancelled the command / hid Done

**RobotLibraryBaseExecuteFB** · `declaration` · variables

```diff
+VAR
+  _executeIn : BOOL;
+  _executeHold : BOOL;
+END_VAR
```

**RobotLibraryBaseExecuteFB** · `body` · patch

```diff
--- a/RobotLibraryBaseExecuteFB.body
+++ b/RobotLibraryBaseExecuteFB.body
@@ -1,2 +1,9 @@
-            SUPER^(AxesGroup := AxesGroup);
+            // ST-FIX F50: a falling edge of Execute must not cancel the command (spec 5.5.x "Output
+// status"): Execute is held internally while Busy; Done/Error/CommandAborted of a command
+// whose Execute is already FALSE are shown for one cycle, then the block resets
+_executeIn := Execute;
+Execute     := Execute OR _executeHold;
+SUPER^(AxesGroup := AxesGroup);
+_executeHold := Busy;
+Execute      := _executeIn;
           
```

### F51

_motion blocks: inputs `AbortingMode`, `SequenceFlag` - checked but not used: the telegram always has the ExecutionMode of the input `ExecMode` → `AbortingMode = ABORT` has no effect (the new command is buffered)_

- F51: AbortingMode/SequenceFlag had no effect on the ExecutionMode of the telegram
- F51: ProcessingMode/SequenceFlag had no effect on the ExecutionMode of the telegram

**MC_BrakeTestFB** · `CreateCommandPayload` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_BrakeTestFB.CreateCommandPayload
+++ b/MC_BrakeTestFB.CreateCommandPayload
@@ -1,6 +1,19 @@
                   // set command parameter 
 _command.CmdTyp               :=  CmdType.BrakeTest;
-_command.ExecMode             :=  ExecMode;
+// ST-FIX F51: ExecutionMode from AbortingMode and SequenceFlag (spec table 5-77)
+IF ( SequenceFlag = SequenceFlag.SECONDARY_SEQUENCE ) THEN
+  IF ( AbortingMode = AbortingMode.ABORT ) THEN
+    _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.SEQUENCE_ABORT_OTHERS_SECONDARY;
+  ELSE
+    _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.SEQUENCE_SECONDARY;
+  END_IF
+ELSE
+  IF ( AbortingMode = AbortingMode.ABORT ) THEN
+    _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.SEQUENCE_ABORT_OTHERS_PRIMARY;
+  ELSE
+    _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.SEQUENCE_PRIMARY;
+  END_IF
+END_IF
 _command.ParSeq               := _command.ParSeq;
 _command.Priority             :=  Priority;
 _command.RobotAxesActive.0    := _parCmd.RobotAxesActive.Bit00;
```

**MC_ActivateNextCommandFB** · `CreateCommandPayload` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_ActivateNextCommandFB.CreateCommandPayload
+++ b/MC_ActivateNextCommandFB.CreateCommandPayload
@@ -1,6 +1,27 @@
                   // set command parameter 
 _command.CmdTyp     :=  CmdType.ActivateNextCommand;
-_command.ExecMode   :=  ExecMode;
+// ST-FIX F51: ExecutionMode from ProcessingMode (and SequenceFlag), spec table 5-77
+CASE ProcessingMode OF
+  ProcessingMode.BUFFERED, ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_BUFFERED:
+    IF ( FALSE ) THEN _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.SEQUENCE_SECONDARY;
+    ELSE _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.SEQUENCE_PRIMARY; END_IF
+  ProcessingMode.ABORTING, ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_ABORTING:
+    IF ( FALSE ) THEN _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.SEQUENCE_ABORT_OTHERS_SECONDARY;
+    ELSE _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.SEQUENCE_ABORT_OTHERS_PRIMARY; END_IF
+  ProcessingMode.PARALLEL, ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_ONCE:
+    _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.PARALLEL;
+  ProcessingMode.CONTINUOUS, ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_CONTINUOUS:
+    _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.CONTINUOUS;
+  ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_MULTIPLE:
+    _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.TRIGGER_MULTIPLE;
+  ProcessingMode.DEACTIVATE:
+    _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.STOP_PARALLEL_CONTINUOUS_TRIGGER;
+ELSE
+  // undefined ProcessingMode -> error, not sent (ST-FIX F49)
+  _command.ExecMode := ExecMode;
+  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_PROCESSINGMODE_NOT_DEFINED, Overwrite := TRUE );
+  OnUpdateStateFlags( State := CmdMessageState.ERROR );
+END_CASE
 _command.ParSeq     := _command.ParSeq;
 _command.Priority   :=  Priority;
 _command.ListenerID := _parCmd.ListenerID;
```

**MC_CollisionDetectionFB** · `CreateCommandPayload` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_CollisionDetectionFB.CreateCommandPayload
+++ b/MC_CollisionDetectionFB.CreateCommandPayload
@@ -1,6 +1,27 @@
                   // set command parameter 
 _command.CmdTyp               :=  CmdType.CollisionDetection;
-_command.ExecMode             :=  ExecMode;
+// ST-FIX F51: ExecutionMode from ProcessingMode (and SequenceFlag), spec table 5-77
+CASE ProcessingMode OF
+  ProcessingMode.BUFFERED, ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_BUFFERED:
+    IF ( SequenceFlag = SequenceFlag.SECONDARY_SEQUENCE ) THEN _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.SEQUENCE_SECONDARY;
+    ELSE _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.SEQUENCE_PRIMARY; END_IF
+  ProcessingMode.ABORTING, ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_ABORTING:
+    IF ( SequenceFlag = SequenceFlag.SECONDARY_SEQUENCE ) THEN _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.SEQUENCE_ABORT_OTHERS_SECONDARY;
+    ELSE _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.SEQUENCE_ABORT_OTHERS_PRIMARY; END_IF
+  ProcessingMode.PARALLEL, ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_ONCE:
+    _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.PARALLEL;
+  ProcessingMode.CONTINUOUS, ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_CONTINUOUS:
+    _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.CONTINUOUS;
+  ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_MULTIPLE:
+    _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.TRIGGER_MULTIPLE;
+  ProcessingMode.DEACTIVATE:
+    _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.STOP_PARALLEL_CONTINUOUS_TRIGGER;
+ELSE
+  // undefined ProcessingMode -> error, not sent (ST-FIX F49)
+  _command.ExecMode := ExecMode;
+  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_PROCESSINGMODE_NOT_DEFINED, Overwrite := TRUE );
+  OnUpdateStateFlags( State := CmdMessageState.ERROR );
+END_CASE
 _command.ParSeq               := _command.ParSeq;
 _command.Priority             :=  Priority;
 
```

```diff
--- a/MC_SetTriggerLimitFB.CreateCommandPayload
+++ b/MC_SetTriggerLimitFB.CreateCommandPayload
@@ -1,6 +1,27 @@
                   // set command parameter 
 _command.CmdTyp                 :=  CmdType.SetTriggerLimit;
-_command.ExecMode               :=  ExecMode;
+// ST-FIX F51: ExecutionMode from ProcessingMode (and SequenceFlag), spec table 5-77
+CASE ProcessingMode OF
+  ProcessingMode.BUFFERED, ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_BUFFERED:
+    IF ( FALSE ) THEN _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.SEQUENCE_SECONDARY;
+    ELSE _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.SEQUENCE_PRIMARY; END_IF
+  ProcessingMode.ABORTING, ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_ABORTING:
+    IF ( FALSE ) THEN _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.SEQUENCE_ABORT_OTHERS_SECONDARY;
+    ELSE _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.SEQUENCE_ABORT_OTHERS_PRIMARY; END_IF
+  ProcessingMode.PARALLEL, ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_ONCE:
+    _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.PARALLEL;
+  ProcessingMode.CONTINUOUS, ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_CONTINUOUS:
+    _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.CONTINUOUS;
+  ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_MULTIPLE:
+    _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.TRIGGER_MULTIPLE;
+  ProcessingMode.DEACTIVATE:
+    _command.ExecMode := ExecutionMode.STOP_PARALLEL_CONTINUOUS_TRIGGER;
+ELSE
+  // undefined ProcessingMode -> error, not sent (ST-FIX F49)
+  _command.ExecMode := ExecMode;
+  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_PROCESSINGMODE_NOT_DEFINED, Overwrite := TRUE );
+  OnUpdateStateFlags( State := CmdMessageState.ERROR );
+END_CASE
 _command.ParSeq                 := _command.ParSeq;
 _command.Priority               :=  Priority;                                
 _command.EmitterID[0]           := _parCmd.EmitterID;
```

### F53

_112 function blocks, `OnExecRun` - `SetTimeout(PT := _timeoutCmd, ...)` is called, but the timer is never evaluated while waiting for the response → no error when a response never arrives (see F52)_

- F53: the command timeout was set but never evaluated
- F53: response state of the last execution must not count for the next one

**RobotLibraryBaseFB** · `body` · patch

```diff
--- a/RobotLibraryBaseFB.body
+++ b/RobotLibraryBaseFB.body
@@ -1,5 +1,16 @@
             OnCall               (AxesGroup := AxesGroup);
 OnExecRun            (AxesGroup := AxesGroup);
+// ST-FIX F53: no response of the RC within _timeoutCmd after the command was added
+// -> error (before: _timerCmd was started but never evaluated, the FB stayed Busy)
+IF ( _uniqueID <> 0 ) AND ( _rspHeader.State = CmdMessageState.EMPTY ) AND ( NOT Error )
+THEN
+  IF ( CheckTimeout( rTimer := _timerCmd ) = RobotLibraryConstants.OK )
+  THEN
+    AxesGroup.Acyclic.ActiveCommandRegister.RemoveCmd( UniqueID := _uniqueID );
+    SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_TIMEOUT_CMD, Overwrite := TRUE );
+    OnUpdateStateFlags( State := CmdMessageState.ERROR );
+  END_IF
+END_IF
 CheckParameterChanged(AxesGroup := AxesGroup);
 
 
```

**RobotLibraryBaseFB** · `Reset` · patch

```diff
--- a/RobotLibraryBaseFB.Reset
+++ b/RobotLibraryBaseFB.Reset
@@ -2,6 +2,7 @@
 _responseReceived := FALSE;
 
 _uniqueId := 0;
+_rspHeader.State := CmdMessageState.EMPTY; // ST-FIX F53: no response yet
 _stepCmd  := 0;
 Error     := FALSE;
 ErrorID   := 0;
```

### F54

_`MC_CreateSplineFB`, `MC_DynamicSplineFB` - loops `FOR _idx := 0 TO SPLINE_DATA_MAX` over `SplineData[1..SPLINE_DATA_MAX]` → access before the array_

- F54: loop 0..SPLINE_DATA_MAX over SplineData[1..SPLINE_DATA_MAX] (reads before the array)

**MC_CreateSplineFB** · `CheckParameterValid` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_CreateSplineFB.CheckParameterValid
+++ b/MC_CreateSplineFB.CheckParameterValid
@@ -22,7 +22,7 @@
   RETURN;                          
 END_IF
 
-FOR _idx := 0 TO RobotLibraryParameter.SPLINE_DATA_MAX
+FOR _idx := 1 TO RobotLibraryParameter.SPLINE_DATA_MAX // ST-FIX F54
 DO
   // Check ParCmd.SplineData[x].Position.X valid ? 
   IF ( SysDepIsValidReal(ParCmd.SplineData[_idx].Position.X) = FALSE ) 
```

**MC_DynamicSplineFB** · `CheckParameterValid` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_DynamicSplineFB.CheckParameterValid
+++ b/MC_DynamicSplineFB.CheckParameterValid
@@ -106,7 +106,7 @@
   RETURN;                          
 END_IF
 
-FOR _idx := 0 TO RobotLibraryParameter.SPLINE_DATA_MAX
+FOR _idx := 1 TO RobotLibraryParameter.SPLINE_DATA_MAX // ST-FIX F54
 DO
   // Check ParCmd.SplineData[x].Position.X valid ? 
   IF ( SysDepIsValidReal(ParCmd.SplineData[_idx].Position.X) = FALSE ) 
```

### F55

_`MC_CreateSplineFB`, `MC_DynamicSplineFB` `.CreateCommandPayload` - the positions of the spline points are never copied into the command → always 0 sent_

- F55: the positions of the spline points were never copied into the command (always 0 sent)

```diff
   _command.SplineData[_idx].ToolNo           :=              _parCmd.SplineData[_idx].ToolNo;
   _command.SplineData[_idx].FrameNo          :=              _parCmd.SplineData[_idx].FrameNo;
+  _command.SplineData[_idx].Position         :=              _parCmd.SplineData[_idx].Position; // ST-FIX F55
   _command.SplineData[_idx].MoveTime         := TIME_TO_UINT(_parCmd.SplineData[_idx].MoveTime);
 END_FOR
 
```

### F57

_`MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleSync*` step 10 - the local change detection compares with `IgnoreTimestamp := FALSE`, but the internal copy holds the timestamp of the RC from the start-up read → every data set counts as changed on the PLC; together with the RC "not in sync" → warning "both sides changed", no synchronisation_

- F57: the internal copy holds the timestamp of the RC (start-up read) -> with the timestamp every data set counted as changed on the PLC (both sides changed, no synchronisation)

**MC_RobotTaskFB** · `HandleSyncToolData` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleSyncToolData
+++ b/MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleSyncToolData
@@ -358,7 +358,7 @@
        FOR _rSyncIdx := 0 TO AxesGroup.State.UnifiedToolIndex
        DO
          // compare tool data
-         AxesGroup.State.DataChanged.Tool[_rSyncIdx] := NOT IsToolDataEqual( Data1 := ToolData[_rSyncIdx].Data, Data2 := _toolData[_rSyncIdx].Data, IgnoreTimestamp := FALSE);
+         AxesGroup.State.DataChanged.Tool[_rSyncIdx] := NOT IsToolDataEqual( Data1 := ToolData[_rSyncIdx].Data, Data2 := _toolData[_rSyncIdx].Data, IgnoreTimestamp := TRUE (* ST-FIX F57 *));
 
          // Check Tool data changed ? 
          IF (AxesGroup.State.DataChanged.Tool[_rSyncIdx])
```

```diff
        DO
          // compare frame data
-         AxesGroup.State.DataChanged.Frame[_rSyncIdx] := NOT IsFrameDataEqual( Data1 := FrameData[_rSyncIdx].Data, Data2 := _frameData[_rSyncIdx].Data, IgnoreTimestamp := FALSE);
+         AxesGroup.State.DataChanged.Frame[_rSyncIdx] := NOT IsFrameDataEqual( Data1 := FrameData[_rSyncIdx].Data, Data2 := _frameData[_rSyncIdx].Data, IgnoreTimestamp := TRUE (* ST-FIX F57 *));

          IF (AxesGroup.State.DataChanged.Frame[_rSyncIdx])
```


### F58

_`MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleSync*` step 10 - data changed on both sides only sets the warning `WARN_*_SYNC_BOTH_SIDES_CHANGED`, also with a fixed direction (`CLIENT_TO_SERVER` / `SERVER_TO_CLIENT`) → synchronisation stops_

- F58: data changed on both sides stopped the synchronisation also with a fixed direction

**MC_RobotTaskFB** · `HandleSyncToolData` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleSyncToolData
+++ b/MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleSyncToolData
@@ -414,7 +414,13 @@
          IF (( NOT AxesGroup.State.SyncStatePlc.InSync.Tool ) AND  
              ( NOT AxesGroup.State.SyncStateRc .InSync.Tool ))
          THEN
-           SetWarning( WarningID := RobotLibraryWarningIdEnum.WARN_TOOL_SYNC_BOTH_SIDES_CHANGED, Overwrite := TRUE);
+           // ST-FIX F58: CLIENT_TO_SERVER / SERVER_TO_CLIENT decide the direction also if both sides changed
+CASE AxesGroup.Parameter.Plc.Parameter.SynchronizationModes.Tool[SyncTime.AFTER_START_UP] OF
+  SyncMode.CLIENT_TO_SERVER : _rStep := 11;
+  SyncMode.SERVER_TO_CLIENT : _rStep := 12;
+ELSE
+  SetWarning( WarningID := RobotLibraryWarningIdEnum.WARN_TOOL_SYNC_BOTH_SIDES_CHANGED, Overwrite := TRUE);
+END_CASE
          END_IF
        END_IF
 
```

### F59

_`MC_RobotTaskFB.AxesGroupFromTelegramCyclicOptional`, `MC_ReadActualPositionCyclicFB.OnExecRun` - the RobotTask writes the **currently used** tool/frame (bytes 36..37 of table 5-89) into `CoordinateSystem`; `ReadActualPositionCyclic` compares it with the requested `ToolNo`/`FrameNo` → as soon as a motion uses another tool the outputs are never updated again (all zero), `Enabled` stays TRUE; the output `CoordinateSystem` is never set_

- F59: the RobotTask put the *currently used* tool/frame into CoordinateSystem and ReadActualPositionCyclic compared it with the requested ToolNo/FrameNo -> no position update when a motion uses another tool (spec 6.1.6, table 5-89); output CoordinateSystem was never set

### F61

_`RobotLibraryBaseFB` (body, `CallBack`, `ParseResponsePayload`) - `Error := ErrorID <> 0` is computed in `OnCall` *before* the parameter check / response evaluation → one cycle `ErrorID ≠ 0` with `Error = FALSE`; with a parameter error `Execute` is TRUE without any of Busy/Done/Error; ACR full (16#8618) leaves `Busy` and `Error` TRUE; GroupJog `Active` and `Error` TRUE; response state ERROR without error code ends the FB without Done and without Error (spec audit B-01…B-05)_

- F61: Error lagged one cycle behind ErrorID; Busy/Active stayed TRUE together with Error
- F61: outputs changed by a response (ErrorID) must be consistent with Error/Busy at once
- F61: response state ERROR without error code ended the FB without Done and without Error

**RobotLibraryBaseFB** · `body` · patch

```diff
--- a/RobotLibraryBaseFB.body
+++ b/RobotLibraryBaseFB.body
@@ -18,4 +18,11 @@
 THEN
   OnOnlineChange(AxesGroup := AxesGroup);
 END_IF
+// ST-FIX F61: Error follows ErrorID in the same cycle, and with Error the other state outputs
+// are reset (Busy/Active/Done/CommandAborted exclusive, spec table 5-45)
+Error := ErrorID <> RobotLibraryConstants.OK;
+IF ( Error )
+THEN
+  OnUpdateStateFlags( State := CmdMessageState.ERROR );
+END_IF
           
```

**RobotLibraryBaseFB** · `CallBack` · patch

```diff
--- a/RobotLibraryBaseFB.CallBack
+++ b/RobotLibraryBaseFB.CallBack
@@ -16,4 +16,11 @@
 
 // set flag for response received
 _responseReceived := TRUE;
+// ST-FIX F61: Error follows ErrorID in the same cycle, and with Error the other state outputs
+// are reset (Busy/Active/Done/CommandAborted exclusive, spec table 5-45)
+Error := ErrorID <> RobotLibraryConstants.OK;
+IF ( Error )
+THEN
+  OnUpdateStateFlags( State := CmdMessageState.ERROR );
+END_IF
                 
```

**RobotLibraryBaseFB** · `ParseResponsePayload` · patch

```diff
--- a/RobotLibraryBaseFB.ParseResponsePayload
+++ b/RobotLibraryBaseFB.ParseResponsePayload
@@ -26,5 +26,10 @@
 
 END_CASE
 
+// ST-FIX F61: state ERROR without error code (or with severity INFO/WARNING) -> 16#8613
+IF ( _rspHeader.State = CmdMessageState.ERROR ) AND ( ErrorID = RobotLibraryConstants.OK )
+THEN
+  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_ROBOT_ERROR_NO_ID, Overwrite := TRUE );
+END_IF
 ParseResponsePayload := ResponseData.PayloadPtr;
                 
```

### F62

_`RobotLibraryBaseFB.ParseResponsePayload` - every response clears `InfoID`/`WarningID`/`ErrorID` (also warnings of the client, e.g. from the parameter check) (B-06)_

- F62: every response cleared InfoID/WarningID/ErrorID (also warnings of the client)

**RobotLibraryBaseFB** · `ParseResponsePayload` · patch

```diff
--- a/RobotLibraryBaseFB.ParseResponsePayload
+++ b/RobotLibraryBaseFB.ParseResponsePayload
@@ -1,7 +1,6 @@
                   // reset message IDs 
-InfoID    := 0;
-WarningID := 0;
-ErrorID   := 0;
+// ST-FIX F62: InfoID/WarningID/ErrorID are held until the falling edge of Execute/Enable
+// (Reset), a response without message must not clear them (spec 5.5.10)
 
 // get State
 _rspHeader.State                := ResponseData.GetHalfeByte1(IncPayloadPtr := FALSE);
```

### F63

_`MC_RobotTaskFB`: `HandleSeqAck`, `AxesGroupToTelegram(Sequence)`, `CheckParameterValid`, telegram coding (`CreateSendPayload(Sequence)`, `ParseRecvPayloadSequence`, `CalculateSequencePayloadMax/StartAdr`) - **two telegram sequences do not work**: the RobotTask never finishes the initialization (A-01); the 1st sequence fills the whole telegram (`SEQUENCE_MAX_PAYLOAD_SIZE` unused, A-02); `CalculateSequencePayloadMax(PRIMARY)` not halved, footer not subtracted (A-03); header/ACK of the 2nd sequence only sent/evaluated sometimes (`SequenceCountSend`, A-04); `_fragIdx` not reset per sequence (A-05); receive order not by Ack (A-06); start values (A-07); `NewSEQ[1]` never reset and the whole PLC→RC telegram cleared when sequence 1 gets a new Seq; no check `Com.TwoSequences` vs `UseTwoSequences` (A-08)_

- F63: two telegram sequences did not work (init hung; areas overlapped; 2nd header/ACK lost)

**MC_RobotTaskFB** · `declaration` · variables

```diff
+VAR
+  /// ST-FIX F63: telegram sequence that is handled/filled first (lower Seq/Ack number)
+  _seqSendFirst : DINT;
+  /// ST-FIX F63: loop counter over the telegram sequences in Seq order
+  _seqOrderIdx : DINT;
+  /// ST-FIX F63: distance of two Seq numbers
+  _seqDiff : DINT;
+  /// ST-FIX F63: next Seq number
+  _seqNext : UINT;
+END_VAR
```

**MC_RobotTaskFB** · `HandleSeqAck` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleSeqAck
+++ b/MC_RobotTaskFB.HandleSeqAck
@@ -1,25 +1,76 @@
-                  FOR _idx := 0 TO  AxesGroup.State.SequenceCountSend

+//  - the sequence with the lower Ack is handled first; its next Seq number is the number of the
+//    other sequence + 1 (one common counter, the sequences alternate)
+//  - ST-FIX F56: 254 -> 1, 0 only on the first exchange
+IF ( _parCfg.Com.TwoSequences )
+THEN
+  AxesGroup.State.SequenceCountSend := 1;
+ELSE
+  AxesGroup.State.SequenceCountSend := 0;
+END_IF
+
+IF (NOT Enable)
+THEN
+  AxesGroup.State.CurrentSEQ[0] := 0;
+  AxesGroup.State.CurrentSEQ[1] := DINT_TO_UINT(AxesGroup.State.SequenceCountSend);
+END_IF
+
+FOR _idx := 0 TO AxesGroup.State.SequenceCountSend
 DO
   AxesGroup.State.CurrentAck[_idx] := Telegram.RobToPlc.Sequence[_idx].Header.SEQ_ACK;

-  IF (NOT Enable)
+END_FOR
+
+  // ST-FIX F63: the sequence with the lower number first (Fig. 5-207/5-208), numbers wrap 254 -> 1

+  IF ( AxesGroup.State.SequenceCountSend = 1 )
   THEN
-    AxesGroup.State.CurrentSEQ[0] := 0;
-    AxesGroup.State.CurrentSEQ[1] := 0;
-  END_IF  
-  
-  // Check Seq/Ack :

+    IF ( _seqDiff < 0 )
+    THEN
+      _seqDiff := _seqDiff + 254;
+    END_IF
+    IF ( _seqDiff > 0 ) AND ( _seqDiff < 127 )
+    THEN
+      _seqSendFirst := 1;

+
+FOR _seqOrderIdx := 0 TO AxesGroup.State.SequenceCountSend
+DO
+  IF ( _seqOrderIdx = 0 )
+  THEN
+    _idx := _seqSendFirst;
+  ELSE
+    _idx := 1 - _seqSendFirst;

-    AxesGroup.State.CurrentSEQ[_idx] := MAX(AxesGroup.State.CurrentSEQ[0],AxesGroup.State.CurrentSEQ[1]) +1;
+    _seqNext := AxesGroup.State.CurrentSEQ[AxesGroup.State.SequenceCountSend - _idx] + 1;
+    IF ( _seqNext >= 255 )
+    THEN
+      _seqNext := 1;
+    END_IF
+    AxesGroup.State.CurrentSEQ[_idx] := _seqNext;
+    AxesGroup.State.NewSEQ[_idx]     := TRUE;
+  END_IF
+END_FOR
 
-    IF (AxesGroup.State.CurrentSEQ[_idx] >= 255) {warning 'ToDo: Test for Yaskawa'}
-    THEN
-      AxesGroup.State.CurrentSEQ[_idx] := 0;
-		END_IF
-    
-    AxesGroup.State.NewSEQ[_idx] := TRUE;
-  END_IF  
-END_FOR
-                
+// order in which the sequences are filled: lower Seq number first
+// ST-FIX F63: the sequence with the lower number first (Fig. 5-207/5-208), numbers wrap 254 -> 1
+_seqSendFirst := 0;
+IF ( AxesGroup.State.SequenceCountSend = 1 )
+THEN
+  _seqDiff := UINT_TO_DINT(AxesGroup.State.CurrentSEQ[0]) - UINT_TO_DINT(AxesGroup.State.CurrentSEQ[1]);
+  IF ( _seqDiff < 0 )

+    _seqDiff := _seqDiff + 254;

+  IF ( _seqDiff > 0 ) AND ( _seqDiff < 127 )

+END_IF
```

_`MC_MoveSplineFB`, `MC_MoveSuperImposedFB`: `OnUpdateStateFlags` - state DONE sets `Done := FALSE` (copy error) → the FB never signals Done, `Execute` TRUE without any status_

- F64: state DONE reset Done instead of setting it -> the FB never signalled Done

```diff
   // Successfully completed
-  CmdMessageState.DONE                : Done               := FALSE;
+  CmdMessageState.DONE                : Done               := TRUE; // ST-FIX F64
                                         Busy               := FALSE;
   // Aborted before completion
```

### F65

_`MC_RobotTaskFB`: `OnCall`, `OnExecRun` step 1, `HandleLifeSign`, `HandleSeqAck` - loss of the initialization always 16#80A2 even if the RC reports the reason in the telegram state (C-01); lifesign timeout of the RC reported as 16#8AAD "connection lost" (C-02); no telegram sequence timeout (C-03)_

- F65: loss of the initialization always 16#80A2, lifesign 16#8AAD, no sequence timeout

**MC_RobotTaskFB** · `declaration` · variables

```diff
+VAR
+  /// ST-FIX F65: telegram sequence timeout (4 x LifeSignTimeOut without new Seq number)
+  _seqAckTimeout : TON;
+  /// ST-FIX F66: lifesign of header and footer differ -> frame is not processed
+  _frameInvalid : BOOL;
+END_VAR
```

**MC_RobotTaskFB** · `OnCall` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_RobotTaskFB.OnCall
+++ b/MC_RobotTaskFB.OnCall
@@ -31,7 +31,19 @@
   // Reset Synchronized flag
   Synchronized := FALSE;
   // Set error 
-  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_INIT_LOST_UNKNOWN_0x80A2, Overwrite := TRUE );
+  // ST-FIX F65: reason of the loss of the initialization (table 7-2): RI error of the RC in the
+  // telegram state (16#A1..16#AD), interface reset by the RC (16#80A7) or unknown (16#80A2)
+  IF (( AxesGroup.Cyclic.RobToPlc.TelegramState >= TelegramState.ERROR_161_TELEGRAM_CONTROL_MISMATCH_TELEGRAM_STATE ) AND
+      ( AxesGroup.Cyclic.RobToPlc.TelegramState <= TelegramState.ERROR_173_SERVER_CONNECTION_LOST                   ))
+  THEN
+    SetError( ErrorID := AxesGroup.Cyclic.RobToPlc.TelegramState, Overwrite := TRUE );
+  ELSIF (( AxesGroup.Cyclic.RobToPlc.TelegramState = TelegramState.READY_FOR_INITIALIZATION ) OR
+         ( AxesGroup.Cyclic.RobToPlc.TelegramState = TelegramState.READY_TO_RESUME          ))
+  THEN
+    SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_INTERFACE_WAS_RESET_AFTER_INIT_0x80A7, Overwrite := TRUE );
+  ELSE
+    SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_INIT_LOST_UNKNOWN_0x80A2, Overwrite := TRUE );
+  END_IF
 END_IF
 
 
```

```diff
     _seqSendFirst := 1;
```


### F69

_`CheckParameterValid` of the FBs with EmitterID / ListenerID / SequenceFlag - EmitterID −128 / ListenerID < 0 reported as 16#0008; ListenerID not checked against the ProcessingMode (> 0 in trigger modes, 0 otherwise); SequenceFlag not checked against the ProcessingMode (D-01…D-03)_

- F69: EmitterID/ListenerID/SequenceFlag were not checked against range and ProcessingMode

**MC_CollisionDetectionFB** · `CheckParameterValid` · patch

```diff
--- a/MC_CollisionDetectionFB.CheckParameterValid
+++ b/MC_CollisionDetectionFB.CheckParameterValid
@@ -38,7 +38,8 @@
 END_IF
 
 // Check SequenceFlag valid ? 
-IF (( SequenceFlag <> SequenceFlagEnum.PRIMARY_SEQUENCE   ) AND
+IF (( SequenceFlag <> SequenceFlagEnum.NO_SEQUENCE        ) AND // ST-FIX F69: default with Parallel
+    ( SequenceFlag <> SequenceFlagEnum.PRIMARY_SEQUENCE   ) AND
     ( SequenceFlag <> SequenceFlagEnum.SECONDARY_SEQUENCE ))
 THEN
   // Parameter not valid
```

**MC_ActivateNextCommandFB** · `CheckParameterValid` · append

```diff
--- a/MC_ActivateNextCommandFB.CheckParameterValid
+++ b/MC_ActivateNextCommandFB.CheckParameterValid
@@ -58,5 +58,16 @@
   RETURN;
 END_IF
 
-
-                
+// ST-FIX F69: trigger IDs and SequenceFlag (table 7-1, 5.5.12.4, e.g. table 6-496)
+IF ( CheckParameterValid ) AND ( ProcessingMode >= ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_BUFFERED ) AND ( ParCmd.ListenerID = 0 )
+THEN
+  CheckParameterValid := FALSE;
+  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_LISTENERID_MUST_BE_GREATER_THAN_ZERO, Overwrite := TRUE );
+  RETURN;
+END_IF
+IF ( CheckParameterValid ) AND ( ProcessingMode <> ProcessingMode.DEACTIVATE ) AND NOT ( ProcessingMode >= ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_BUFFERED ) AND ( ParCmd.ListenerID > 0 )
+THEN
+  CheckParameterValid := FALSE;
+  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_LISTENERID_NOT_ALLOWED, Overwrite := TRUE );
+  RETURN;
+END_IF
```

**MC_CallSubprogramFB** · `CheckParameterValid` · append

```diff
--- a/MC_CallSubprogramFB.CheckParameterValid
+++ b/MC_CallSubprogramFB.CheckParameterValid
@@ -124,4 +124,23 @@
     RETURN;                          
   END_IF
 END_FOR
-                
+
+// ST-FIX F69: trigger IDs and SequenceFlag (table 7-1, 5.5.12.4, e.g. table 6-496)
+IF ( CheckParameterValid ) AND ( ProcessingMode >= ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_BUFFERED ) AND ( ParCmd.ListenerID = 0 )
+THEN
+  CheckParameterValid := FALSE;
+  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_LISTENERID_MUST_BE_GREATER_THAN_ZERO, Overwrite := TRUE );
+  RETURN;
+END_IF
+IF ( CheckParameterValid ) AND ( ProcessingMode <> ProcessingMode.DEACTIVATE ) AND NOT ( ProcessingMode >= ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_BUFFERED ) AND ( ParCmd.ListenerID > 0 )
+THEN
+  CheckParameterValid := FALSE;
+  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_LISTENERID_NOT_ALLOWED, Overwrite := TRUE );
+  RETURN;
+END_IF
+IF ( CheckParameterValid ) AND ( ( (( ProcessingMode = ProcessingMode.BUFFERED ) OR ( ProcessingMode = ProcessingMode.ABORTING ) OR ( ProcessingMode = ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_BUFFERED ) OR ( ProcessingMode = ProcessingMode.TRIGGER_ABORTING )) ) = ( SequenceFlag = SequenceFlag.NO_SEQUENCE ) )
+THEN
+  CheckParameterValid := FALSE;
+  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_SEQFLAG_INVALID_IN_PROC_MODE, Overwrite := TRUE );
+  RETURN;
+END_IF
```

**RobotLibraryBaseExecuteFB** · `OnExecStart` · append

```diff
--- a/RobotLibraryBaseExecuteFB.OnExecStart
+++ b/RobotLibraryBaseExecuteFB.OnExecStart
@@ -1,2 +1,10 @@
-                  
-                
+// ST-FIX F71: Priority (1 = very high ... 4 = low, table 7-1)
+IF ( Priority < PriorityLevel.VERY_HIGH )
+THEN
+  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_PRIORITY_TOO_HIGH, Overwrite := TRUE );
+  Error := TRUE;
+ELSIF ( Priority > PriorityLevel.LOW )
+THEN
+  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_PRIORITY_TOO_LOW, Overwrite := TRUE );
+  Error := TRUE;
+END_IF
```

### F72

_`MC_CallSubprogramFB.CheckParameterValid` - acyclic data > 190 bytes not rejected (D-09)_

- F72: CallSubprogram: AcyclicData longer than 190 bytes not rejected (16#8416)

**MC_CallSubprogramFB** · `CheckParameterValid` · append

```diff
--- a/MC_CallSubprogramFB.CheckParameterValid
+++ b/MC_CallSubprogramFB.CheckParameterValid
@@ -144,3 +144,11 @@
   SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_SEQFLAG_INVALID_IN_PROC_MODE, Overwrite := TRUE );
   RETURN;
 END_IF
+
+// ST-FIX F72: at most 190 bytes of acyclic data (table 7-1)
+IF ( CheckParameterValid ) AND ( UPPER_BOUND(ParCmd.Data, 1) - LOWER_BOUND(ParCmd.Data, 1) + 1 > 190 )
+THEN
+  CheckParameterValid := FALSE;
+  SetError( ErrorID := RobotLibraryErrorIdEnum.ERR_ACYCLICDATA_TOO_LARGE, Overwrite := TRUE );
+  RETURN;
+END_IF
```

### F74

_`RobotLibraryBaseFB.OnCall`, `AxesGroupMessageLogFB.AddMessageLog`, `AlarmMessage` - messages of the command only with the severity of the last RC response → client-side errors (parameter check) never in the message buffer; `AcrID`/`CmdType` missing (E-01, E-03)_

- F74: client-side command messages were not written into the message buffer; no AcrID/CmdType

**RobotLibraryBaseFB** · `declaration` · variables

```diff
+VAR
+  /// ST-FIX F74: message of the command for the message buffer
+  _alarmMessage : AlarmMessage;
+END_VAR
```

**AxesGroupMessageLogFB** · `AddMessageLog` · patch

```diff
--- a/AxesGroupMessageLogFB.AddMessageLog
+++ b/AxesGroupMessageLogFB.AddMessageLog
@@ -48,6 +48,8 @@
   Messages[0].Severity    := MessageLog.Severity;
   Messages[0].MessageText := MessageLog.MessageText;
   Messages[0].MessageCode := MessageLog.MessageCode;
+  Messages[0].AcrID       := MessageLog.AcrID;   // ST-FIX F74
+  Messages[0].CmdType     := MessageLog.CmdType; // ST-FIX F74
   
   // inc message counter
   MessagesEntries := DINT_TO_UINT(LIMIT(0, MessagesEntries + 1, RobotLibraryParameter.MESSAGE_LOG_MAX));
```

**RobotLibraryBaseFB** · `OnCall` · patch

```diff
--- a/RobotLibraryBaseFB.OnCall
+++ b/RobotLibraryBaseFB.OnCall
@@ -15,20 +15,55 @@
 _info_R   ( CLK := InfoID    <> RobotLibraryConstants.OK );
 
 
-// Log Command events to message log 
-IF  ( LogLevel < _rspHeader.AlarmMessageSeverity ) AND
-   (( _error_R  .Q                               ) OR 
-    ( _warning_R.Q                               ) OR 
-    ( _info_R   .Q                               ))
+// ST-FIX F74: every message of the command - also the client-side ones (e.g. parameter errors,
+// before: only with the severity of the last response of the RC) - goes into the message
+// buffer, with ACR entry and command type
+IF ( _error_R.Q ) AND ( LogLevel < Severity.ERROR )
 THEN
-  // Add command message to message buffer       
-  AxesGroup.MessageLog
-    .AddMessageLogByParameter(  Timestamp   :=   AxesGroup.State.SystemTime,
-                                MessageType :=   MessageType.CMD,
-                                MessageCode :=  _rspHeader.AlarmMessageCode,
-                                MessageText :=   CONCAT(MyType, CONCAT(' : ' , MESSAGE_CODE_TO_STRING(_rspHeader.AlarmMessageCode))),
-                                Severity    :=  _rspHeader.AlarmMessageSeverity ); 
-END_IF 
+  _alarmMessage.Severity    := Severity.ERROR;
+  IF ( ErrorID = _rspHeader.AlarmMessageCode ) AND ( _rspHeader.AlarmMessageSeverity > Severity.ERROR )
+  THEN
+    _alarmMessage.Severity  := _rspHeader.AlarmMessageSeverity; // e.g. fatal error of the RC
+  END_IF
+  _alarmMessage.Timestamp   := AxesGroup.State.SystemTime;
+  _alarmMessage.MessageType := MessageType.CMD;
+  _alarmMessage.MessageCode := ErrorID;
+  _alarmMessage.AcrID       := _uniqueID;
+  _alarmMessage.CmdType     := _cmdHeader.CmdTyp;
+  _alarmMessage.MessageText := CONCAT(MyType, CONCAT(' : ', MESSAGE_CODE_TO_STRING(ErrorID)));
+  AxesGroup.MessageLog.AddMessageLog( MessageLog := _alarmMessage );
+END_IF
+IF ( _warning_R.Q ) AND ( LogLevel < Severity.WARNING )
+THEN
+  _alarmMessage.Severity    := Severity.WARNING;
+  IF ( WarningID = _rspHeader.AlarmMessageCode ) AND ( _rspHeader.AlarmMessageSeverity > Severity.WARNING )
+  THEN
+    _alarmMessage.Severity  := _rspHeader.AlarmMessageSeverity; // e.g. fatal error of the RC
+  END_IF
+  _alarmMessage.Timestamp   := AxesGroup.State.SystemTime;
+  _alarmMessage.MessageType := MessageType.CMD;
+  _alarmMessage.MessageCode := WarningID;
+  _alarmMessage.AcrID       := _uniqueID;
+  _alarmMessage.CmdType     := _cmdHeader.CmdTyp;
+  _alarmMessage.MessageText := CONCAT(MyType, CONCAT(' : ', MESSAGE_CODE_TO_STRING(WarningID)));
+  AxesGroup.MessageLog.AddMessageLog( MessageLog := _alarmMessage );
+END_IF
+IF ( _info_R.Q ) AND ( LogLevel < Severity.INFO )
+THEN
+  _alarmMessage.Severity    := Severity.INFO;
+  IF ( InfoID = _rspHeader.AlarmMessageCode ) AND ( _rspHeader.AlarmMessageSeverity > Severity.INFO )
+  THEN
+    _alarmMessage.Severity  := _rspHeader.AlarmMessageSeverity; // e.g. fatal error of the RC
+  END_IF
+  _alarmMessage.Timestamp   := AxesGroup.State.SystemTime;
+  _alarmMessage.MessageType := MessageType.CMD;
+  _alarmMessage.MessageCode := InfoID;
+  _alarmMessage.AcrID       := _uniqueID;
+  _alarmMessage.CmdType     := _cmdHeader.CmdTyp;
+  _alarmMessage.MessageText := CONCAT(MyType, CONCAT(' : ', MESSAGE_CODE_TO_STRING(InfoID)));
+  AxesGroup.MessageLog.AddMessageLog( MessageLog := _alarmMessage );
+END_IF
+ 
 
 
 IF ( _error_R.Q)
```

## Data types

Change the DUTs / enums / constants of the library:

- Constant `RobotLibraryConstants.SRCIVersion` := `(MajorVersion := 1, MinorVersion := 5, PatchVersion := 0)` - PLC library still says 1.3.0, but implements SRCI 1.5 (SDK: SRCI_VERSION 1.5)
- **F32** New field in `GroupStopRecvData` (at the end): `AbortedSequence : SINT;  /// Sequence whose commands were aborted` - ST-FIX F32: byte 4 of the response (spec GroupStop)
- **F32** New field in `ExchangeConfigurationRecvData` (at the end): `NumberOfServerLogs : UINT;  /// Number of server log entries` - ST-FIX F32: bytes 28..29 of the response
- **F32** New field in `ExchangeConfigurationOutCmd` (at the end): `NumberOfServerLogs : UINT;  /// Number of server log entries` - ST-FIX F32
- **F32** New field in `ReadRobotSWLimitsOutCmd` (at the end): `DataChanged : BOOL;  /// The limits were changed on the RC since the last synchronization` - ST-FIX F32: byte 106 of the response
- **F35** Enum `CmdType.MoveCircularAbsolute` := 2106 (add the element if missing) - F35: spec 6.3.13 Type 2106 (library 2109)
- **F35** Enum `CmdType.MoveCircularRelative` := 2107 (add the element if missing) - F35: spec Type 2107 (library 2106)
- **F35** Enum `CmdType.MoveLinearAbsoluteJ` := 2109 (add the element if missing) - F35: spec 6.3.12 Type 2109 (missing in the library)
- **F35** Enum `CmdType.SoftSwitchTcp` := 7300 (add the element if missing) - F35: spec Type 7300 (missing in the library)
- **F41** Field `VelocityRate` (initial value `-1.0`) in `MoveApproachDirectParCmd`, `MoveApproachLinearParCmd`, `MoveAxesAbsoluteParCmd`, `MoveAxesRelativeParCmd`, `MoveCircularAbsoluteParCmd`, `MoveCircularCamParCmd`, `MoveCircularRelativeParCmd`, `MoveDepartDirectParCmd`, `MoveDepartLinearParCmd`, `MoveDirectAbsoluteParCmd`, `MoveDirectOffsetParCmd`, `MoveDirectRelativeParCmd`, `MoveLinearAbsoluteJParCmd`, `MoveLinearAbsoluteParCmd`, `MoveLinearCamParCmd`, `MoveLinearOffsetParCmd`, `MoveLinearRelativeParCmd`, `MovePickPlaceDirectParCmd`, `MovePickPlaceLinearParCmd`, `ReturnToPrimaryParCmd`, `SearchHardStopJParCmd`, `SearchHardStopParCmd` - ST-FIX F41: spec 5.x robot dynamics parameter: '<0 %: use default' (default); the library had 0.0 = internal minimal value (the RC rejects 0 for DecelerationRate/JerkRate)
- **F41** Field `AccelerationRate` (initial value `-1.0`) in `MoveApproachDirectParCmd`, `MoveApproachLinearParCmd`, `MoveAxesAbsoluteParCmd`, `MoveAxesRelativeParCmd`, `MoveCircularAbsoluteParCmd`, `MoveCircularCamParCmd`, `MoveCircularRelativeParCmd`, `MoveDepartDirectParCmd`, `MoveDepartLinearParCmd`, `MoveDirectAbsoluteParCmd`, `MoveDirectOffsetParCmd`, `MoveDirectRelativeParCmd`, `MoveLinearAbsoluteJParCmd`, `MoveLinearAbsoluteParCmd`, `MoveLinearCamParCmd`, `MoveLinearOffsetParCmd`, `MoveLinearRelativeParCmd`, `MovePickPlaceDirectParCmd`, `MovePickPlaceLinearParCmd`, `ReturnToPrimaryParCmd`, `SearchHardStopJParCmd`, `SearchHardStopParCmd` - ST-FIX F41: spec 5.x robot dynamics parameter: '<0 %: use default' (default); the library had 0.0 = internal minimal value (the RC rejects 0 for DecelerationRate/JerkRate)
- **F41** Field `DecelerationRate` (initial value `-1.0`) in `MoveApproachDirectParCmd`, `MoveApproachLinearParCmd`, `MoveAxesAbsoluteParCmd`, `MoveAxesRelativeParCmd`, `MoveCircularAbsoluteParCmd`, `MoveCircularCamParCmd`, `MoveCircularRelativeParCmd`, `MoveDepartDirectParCmd`, `MoveDepartLinearParCmd`, `MoveDirectAbsoluteParCmd`, `MoveDirectOffsetParCmd`, `MoveDirectRelativeParCmd`, `MoveLinearAbsoluteJParCmd`, `MoveLinearAbsoluteParCmd`, `MoveLinearCamParCmd`, `MoveLinearOffsetParCmd`, `MoveLinearRelativeParCmd`, `MovePickPlaceDirectParCmd`, `MovePickPlaceLinearParCmd`, `ReturnToPrimaryParCmd`, `SearchHardStopJParCmd`, `SearchHardStopParCmd` - ST-FIX F41: spec 5.x robot dynamics parameter: '<0 %: use default' (default); the library had 0.0 = internal minimal value (the RC rejects 0 for DecelerationRate/JerkRate)
- **F41** Field `JerkRate` (initial value `-1.0`) in `MoveApproachDirectParCmd`, `MoveApproachLinearParCmd`, `MoveAxesAbsoluteParCmd`, `MoveAxesRelativeParCmd`, `MoveCircularAbsoluteParCmd`, `MoveCircularCamParCmd`, `MoveCircularRelativeParCmd`, `MoveDepartDirectParCmd`, `MoveDepartLinearParCmd`, `MoveDirectAbsoluteParCmd`, `MoveDirectOffsetParCmd`, `MoveDirectRelativeParCmd`, `MoveLinearAbsoluteJParCmd`, `MoveLinearAbsoluteParCmd`, `MoveLinearCamParCmd`, `MoveLinearOffsetParCmd`, `MoveLinearRelativeParCmd`, `MovePickPlaceDirectParCmd`, `MovePickPlaceLinearParCmd`, `ReturnToPrimaryParCmd`, `SearchHardStopJParCmd`, `SearchHardStopParCmd` - ST-FIX F41: spec 5.x robot dynamics parameter: '<0 %: use default' (default); the library had 0.0 = internal minimal value (the RC rejects 0 for DecelerationRate/JerkRate)
- **F44** Field `LifeSignTimeOut` (initial value `50`) in `ExchangeConfigurationParCmd` - ST-FIX F44: spec 5.6.6: LifeSignTimeOut default 50 ms (min 10 ms); the library had 0 (invalid)
- **F45** New field in `CallSubprogramOutCmd` (at the end): `Progress : UINT;  /// Progress of the subprogram [%] (spec output Progress)` - ST-FIX F45
- **F45** New field in `SetTriggerLimitOutCmd` (at the end): `Data : ARRAY[0..11] OF REAL;  /// Actual values of the monitored data (spec output Data)` - ST-FIX F45
- **F46** Field `OriginID` (comment `/// OriginID of the stopped subprogram (spec 5.5.12.4 EmitterID, ListenerID, FollowID and OriginID)`) in `StopSubprogramOutCmd` - ST-FIX F46: comment missing
- **F46** Field `RCInterpreterVersion` (comment `/// Version of the server implementation in the format X.X.X (spec table 6-18)`) in `ReadRobotDataOutCmd` - ST-FIX F46: comment missing
- **F52** Constant `RobotLibraryParameter.FRAGMENT_MAX` := `31` - ST-FIX F52: 10 fragments (0..9) per telegram are too few - a 256 byte telegram holds up to 27 fragments (header 8 bytes + at least 1 byte payload); fragments behind the array were dropped although the telegram was acknowledged -> responses lost
- **F59** New field in `AxesGroupCyclicOptionalDataCartesianPosition` (after `CoordinateSystem`): `CurrentCoordinateSystem : RobotCoordinateSystemParameters;  /// Tool and frame currently used by the RC (bytes 36..37 of the optional cyclic Cartesian position)` - ST-FIX F59: CoordinateSystem is the tool/frame of the returned position (bytes 34..35), the currently used tool/frame needs an own field (spec table 5-89, 6.1.6)
